No traceback or exception in the log. The only errors are the initial ReadTimeout warnings from HuggingFace which were retried and succeeded. The process seems to have been killed without leaving any error message in the log — this suggests an OOM kill or the AutoDL instance was restarted/killed.

Looking at the log modification time: 11:07:46. The instance seems to have restarted at 11:08 (tensorboard/jupyter started at 11:08). This strongly suggests the AutoDL instance was restarted, killing the exp-016 process.

Let me check if there's a `depth_4.jsonl` partial file or if the data was being written to memory before flushing.


I should also verify the machine uptime to confirm when it was last restarted.
